</td>
</tr></table></div>
</div>
<div class="sect2">
<h3 id="specify-both-audio-and-video-output-devices">
<a class="anchor" href="#specify-both-audio-and-video-output-devices"></a>Specify both audio and video output devices</h3>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>You can combine audio and video output options. For example, to direct video output to the touchscreen, and audio output to the headphone jack, use the following combination of the commands above:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span>cvlc <span class="nt">--play-and-exit</span> <span class="nt">--fullscreen</span> <span class="nt">--drm-vout-display</span> DSI-1 <span class="nt">-A</span> alsa <span class="nt">--alsa-audio-device</span> sysdefault:CARD<span class="o">=</span>Headphones your_video.mp4</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect2">
<h3 id="improve-stream-playback-performance">
<a class="anchor" href="#improve-stream-playback-performance"></a>Improve stream playback performance</h3>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>If you have a raw H.264 stream, like those captured from a Raspberry Pi Camera Module, you can improve playback performance in VLC by wrapping the stream inside a container format such as MP4. You can use <code>ffmpeg</code> to convert stream content into a container file. For example, the following command converts a stream named <code>video.h264</code> to a MP4 container named <code>video.mp4</code> at 30fps:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span>ffmpeg <span class="nt">-r</span> 30 <span class="nt">-i</span> video.h264 <span class="nt">-c</span>:v copy video.mp4</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect1">
<h2 id="utilities">
<a class="anchor" href="#utilities"></a>Utilities</h2>
<div class="sectionbody"></div>
<div class="paragraph edit-link">
<p>Edit this <a href="https://github.com/raspberrypi/documentation/blob/master/documentation/asciidoc/computers/os/graphics-utilities.adoc">on GitHub</a></p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>There are several useful command-line utilities pre-installed in Raspberry Pi OS.</p>
</div>
<div class="sect2">
<h3 id="kmsprint">
<a class="anchor" href="#kmsprint"></a><code>kmsprint</code>
</h3>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>The <code>kmsprint</code> tool can be used to list the display-modes supported by the monitors attached to your Raspberry Pi device.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Use <code>kmsprint</code> to see the state of all displays connected to your Raspberry Pi, and <code>kmsprint -m</code> to list the display modes supported by each display.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>On Raspberry Pi 5 and later, display interfaces (such as HDMI, DSI, DPI, and VEC) are exposed across multiple separate DRM cards (/dev/dri/cardX), rather than on a single unified card as on Raspberry Pi 4 and earlier.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>To query a specific interface on Raspberry Pi 5, you might need to specify the device node using the <code>--device</code> (or <code>-d</code>) option:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span>kmsprint <span class="nt">--device</span><span class="o">=</span>/dev/dri/card1</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Card numbers are assigned dynamically at boot and can change between restarts depending on the attached hardware.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect2">
<h3 id="vclog">
<a class="anchor" href="#vclog"></a><code>vclog</code>
</h3>
<div class="paragraph">
<p><code>vclog</code> displays log messages from the VideoCore GPU from Linux running on the Arm. It needs to be run as root.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p><code>sudo vclog --msg</code> prints out the message log, while <code>sudo vclog --assert</code> prints out the assertion log.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect2">
<h3 id="vcgencmd">
<a class="anchor" href="#vcgencmd"></a><code>vcgencmd</code>
</h3>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>The <code>vcgencmd</code> tool is used to output information from the VideoCore GPU on Raspberry Pi devices. You can find source code for the <code>vcgencmd</code> utility <a href="https://github.com/raspberrypi/utils/tree/master/vcgencmd">on GitHub</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>To get a list of all commands supported by <code>vcgencmd</code>, use <code>vcgencmd commands</code>. Some useful commands and their required parameters are listed here:</p>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="vcos">
<a class="anchor" href="#vcos"></a><code>vcos</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>The <code>vcos</code> command has two useful sub-commands:</p>
</div>
<div class="ulist">
<ul><li>
<p><code>version</code> displays the build date and version of the firmware on the VideoCore</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>log status</code> displays the error log status of the various VideoCore firmware areas</p>
</li>
</ul></div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="version">
<a class="anchor" href="#version"></a><code>version</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Displays the build date and version of the VideoCore firmware.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="get_throttled">
<a class="anchor" href="#get_throttled"></a><code>get_throttled</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Returns the throttled state of the system. This is a bit pattern. A bit being set indicates the following meanings:</p>
</div>
<table class="tableblock frame-all grid-all stretch"><colgroup><col style="width: 33.3333%;"><col style="width: 33.3333%;"><col style="width: 33.3334%;"></colgroup><thead><tr><th class="tableblock halign-center valign-top">Bit</th>
<th class="tableblock halign-left valign-top">Hexadecimal value</th>
<th class="tableblock halign-left valign-top">Meaning</th>
</tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock">0</p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>0x1</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Undervoltage detected</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock">1</p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>0x2</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Arm frequency capped</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock">2</p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>0x4</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Currently throttled</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock">3</p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>0x8</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Soft temperature limit active</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock">16</p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>0x10000</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Undervoltage has occurred</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock">17</p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>0x20000</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Arm frequency capping has occurred</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock">18</p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>0x40000</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Throttling has occurred</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock">19</p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>0x80000</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Soft temperature limit has occurred</p></td>
</tr></tbody></table></div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="measure_temp">
<a class="anchor" href="#measure_temp"></a><code>measure_temp</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Returns the temperature of the SoC as measured by its internal temperature sensor.
On Raspberry Pi 4 family and Raspberry Pi 5 family devices, <code>measure_temp pmic</code> returns the temperature of the PMIC.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="measure_clock-clock">
<a class="anchor" href="#measure_clock-clock"></a><code>measure_clock [clock]</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>This returns the current frequency of the specified clock. Accepts the following clock values:</p>
</div>
<table class="tableblock frame-all grid-all stretch"><colgroup><col style="width: 50%;"><col style="width: 50%;"></colgroup><thead><tr><th class="tableblock halign-center valign-top">clock</th>
<th class="tableblock halign-left valign-top">Description</th>
</tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>arm</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Arm core(s)</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>core</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">GPU core</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>h264</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">H.264 block</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>isp</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Image Sensor Pipeline</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>v3d</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">3D block</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>uart</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">UART</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>pwm</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">PWM block (analogue audio output)</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>emmc</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">SD card interface</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>pixel</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Pixel valves</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>vec</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Analogue video encoder</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>hdmi</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">HDMI</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>dpi</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">Display Parallel Interface</p></td>
</tr></tbody></table><div class="paragraph">
<p>For example, <code>vcgencmd measure_clock arm</code></p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="measure_volts-block">
<a class="anchor" href="#measure_volts-block"></a><code>measure_volts [block]</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Displays the current voltages used by the specific block. Accepts the following block values:</p>
</div>
<table class="tableblock frame-all grid-all stretch"><colgroup><col style="width: 50%;"><col style="width: 50%;"></colgroup><thead><tr><th class="tableblock halign-center valign-top">block</th>
<th class="tableblock halign-left valign-top">Description</th>
</tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>core</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">VC4 core voltage</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>sdram_c</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">SDRAM Core Voltage</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>sdram_i</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">SDRAM I/O voltage</p></td>
</tr><tr><td class="tableblock halign-center valign-top"><p class="tableblock"><code>sdram_p</code></p></td>
<td class="tableblock halign-left valign-top"><p class="tableblock">SDRAM Phy Voltage</p></td>
</tr></tbody></table></div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="pmic_read_adc-channel">
<a class="anchor" href="#pmic_read_adc-channel"></a><code>pmic_read_adc [channel]</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>On Raspberry Pi 5, displays the readings of the ADCs built into the PMIC: the current drawn from, and the voltage of, each PMIC supply rail, plus the supply voltage <code>EXT5V_V</code>. Without a parameter, the command lists every channel:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span>vcgencmd pmic_read_adc
<span class="go"> 3V7_WL_SW_A current(0)=0.09466522A
   3V3_SYS_A current(1)=0.05465208A
   1V8_SYS_A current(2)=0.16005250A
  DDR_VDD2_A current(3)=0.02441920A
  DDR_VDDQ_A current(4)=0.00000000A
   1V1_SYS_A current(5)=0.17566740A
    0V8_SW_A current(6)=0.26838080A
  VDD_CORE_A current(7)=0.51794000A
   3V3_DAC_A current(17)=0.00000000A
   3V3_ADC_A current(18)=0.00048840A
   0V8_AON_A current(16)=0.00293040A
      HDMI_A current(22)=0.02576310A
 3V7_WL_SW_V volt(8)=3.70627200V
   3V3_SYS_V volt(9)=3.31188900V
   1V8_SYS_V volt(10)=1.80268400V
  DDR_VDD2_V volt(11)=1.11355200V
  DDR_VDDQ_V volt(12)=0.60549390V
   1V1_SYS_V volt(13)=1.11025500V
    0V8_SW_V volt(14)=0.80402850V
  VDD_CORE_V volt(15)=0.75186740V
   3V3_DAC_V volt(20)=3.31409900V
   3V3_ADC_V volt(21)=3.30768900V
   0V8_AON_V volt(19)=0.80146450V
      HDMI_V volt(23)=5.06654000V
     EXT5V_V volt(24)=5.06788000V
      BATT_V volt(25)=0.00085470V</span></code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>To read a single channel, pass its name. For example, to read the supply voltage:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span>vcgencmd pmic_read_adc EXT5V_V
<span class="go">     EXT5V_V volt(24)=5.06520000V</span></code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>If the channel name isn’t recognised, the command lists every channel instead of returning an error.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>These readings don’t include USB current or anything else connected directly to the 5V supply, because that power bypasses the PMIC. For more information, see <a href="raspberry-pi.html#power-supplies-and-raspberry-pi-os">Power supplies and Raspberry Pi OS</a>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="otp_dump">
<a class="anchor" href="#otp_dump"></a><code>otp_dump</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Displays the content of the OTP (one-time programmable) memory inside the SoC. These are 32-bit values, indexed from 8 to 64. See the <a href="raspberry-pi.html#otp-register-and-bit-definitions">OTP bits page</a> for more details.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="getconfig">
<a class="anchor" href="#getconfig"></a><code>get_config [configuration item|int|str]</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Displays the value of the configuration setting specified; alternatively, specify either <code>int</code> (integer) or <code>str</code> (string) to see all configuration items of the given type. For example, the following command returns the total memory on the device in megabytes:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span>vcgencmd get_config total_mem</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="get_mem-type">
<a class="anchor" href="#get_mem-type"></a><code>get_mem type</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Reports on the amount of memory addressable by the Arm and the GPU. To show the amount of Arm-addressable memory, use <code>vcgencmd get_mem arm</code>; to show the amount of GPU-addressable memory, use <code>vcgencmd get_mem gpu</code>. On devices with more than 1 GB of memory, the <code>arm</code> parameter always returns 1 GB minus the <code>gpu</code> memory value, since the GPU firmware is only aware of the first 1 GB of memory. To get an accurate report of the total memory on the device, see the <code>total_mem</code> configuration item and the <a href="#getconfig"><code>get_config</code></a> section earlier.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="codec_enabled-type">
<a class="anchor" href="#codec_enabled-type"></a><code>codec_enabled [type]</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Reports whether the specified codec type is enabled. Possible options for type are AGIF, FLAC, H263, H264, MJPA, MJPB, MJPG, MPG2, MPG4, MVC0, PCM, THRA, VORB, VP6, VP8, WMV9, WVC1. Because the H.265 HW block on the Raspberry Pi 4 and later device families isn’t part of the VideoCore GPU, its status isn’t accessed by this command.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="mem_oom">
<a class="anchor" href="#mem_oom"></a><code>mem_oom</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Displays statistics on any OOM (out of memory) events occurring in the VideoCore memory space.
This command isn’t available on Raspberry Pi 5.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="mem_reloc_stats">
<a class="anchor" href="#mem_reloc_stats"></a><code>mem_reloc_stats</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Displays statistics from the relocatable memory allocator on the VideoCore.
This command isn’t available on Raspberry Pi 5.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="read_ring_osc">
<a class="anchor" href="#read_ring_osc"></a><code>read_ring_osc</code>
</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Returns the current speed, voltage, and temperature of the ring oscillator.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect1">
<h2 id="accessibility-options">
<a class="anchor" href="#accessibility-options"></a>Accessibility options</h2>
<div class="sectionbody"></div>
<div class="paragraph edit-link">
<p>Edit this <a href="https://github.com/raspberrypi/documentation/blob/master/documentation/asciidoc/computers/os/accessibility.adoc">on GitHub</a></p>
</div>
<div class="sect2">
<h3 id="visual-aids">
<a class="anchor" href="#visual-aids"></a>Visual aids</h3>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Users of Raspberry Pi OS with visual impairments can find helpful tools in the <strong>Recommended Software</strong> menu.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>We offer <a href="https://help.gnome.org/users/orca/stable/introduction.html.en">Orca screen reader</a> to ease navigation of Raspberry Pi Desktop, and a screen magnifier to increase the readability of UI and screen elements. You can also configure the size of UI elements from <strong>Control Centre</strong>.</p>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="orca-screen-reader">
<a class="anchor" href="#orca-screen-reader"></a>Orca screen reader</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>You can install Orca screen reader from the <strong>Recommended Software</strong> section of the main Raspberry Pi menu. Alternatively, press <strong>Ctrl</strong> + <strong>Alt</strong> + <strong>Space</strong> to automatically install Orca.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>When booting Raspberry Pi OS for the first time after installing a new image, an automatic spoken reminder plays after 30 seconds. This reminder provides instructions on how to install Orca.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="screen-magnifier">
<a class="anchor" href="#screen-magnifier"></a>Screen magnifier</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>To magnify your screen, press  <strong>Ctrl</strong> + <strong>Alt</strong> + <strong>M</strong>. This increases the size of the displayed desktop and, as a result, only a portion of it is now visible on screen. Move your mouse cursor around to view different parts of the magnified desktop. To turn off screen magnification, press  <strong>Ctrl</strong> + <strong>Alt</strong> + <strong>M</strong>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="enlarge-ui-elements">
<a class="anchor" href="#enlarge-ui-elements"></a>Enlarge UI elements</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>To enlarge the UI elements for easier viewing, select <strong>Preferences &gt; Control Centre</strong>, then go to the <strong>Defaults</strong> tab. On this tab, you can choose to <strong>Set Defaults</strong> for different sizes of screen. The larger the screen that you select <strong>Set Defaults</strong> for, the larger the UI text, icons, and other elements appear.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect1">
<h2 id="use-python-on-a-raspberry-pi">
<a class="anchor" href="#use-python-on-a-raspberry-pi"></a>Use Python on a Raspberry Pi</h2>
<div class="sectionbody"></div>
<div class="paragraph edit-link">
<p>Edit this <a href="https://github.com/raspberrypi/documentation/blob/master/documentation/asciidoc/computers/os/using-python.adoc">on GitHub</a></p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Raspberry Pi OS comes with Python 3 pre-installed. Interfering with the system Python installation can cause problems for your operating system. When you install third-party Python libraries, always use the correct package-management tools.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>On Linux, you can install <code>python</code> dependencies in two ways:</p>
</div>
<div class="ulist">
<ul><li>
<p>use <code>apt</code> to install pre-configured system packages</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>use <code>pip</code> to install libraries using Python’s dependency manager <em>in a virtual environment</em></p>
<div class="admonitionblock important">
<table><tr><td class="icon">
<div class="title">Important</div>
</td>
<td class="content">
From Raspberry Pi OS <em>Bookworm</em> onwards, you can only use <code>pip</code> to install into a Python Virtual Environment (<code>venv</code>). This change was introduced by the Python community, not by Raspberry Pi: for more information, see <a href="https://peps.python.org/pep-0668/">PEP 668</a>.
</td>
</tr></table></div>
</li>
</ul></div>
<div class="sect2">
<h3 id="install-python-packages-using-apt">
<a class="anchor" href="#install-python-packages-using-apt"></a>Install Python packages using <code>apt</code>
</h3>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Packages installed through <code>apt</code> are packaged specifically for Raspberry Pi OS. These packages usually come pre-compiled, so they install faster. Because <code>apt</code> manages dependencies for all packages, installing with this method includes all of the sub-dependencies needed to run the package. And <code>apt</code> ensures that you don’t break other packages if you uninstall.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>For instance, to install the Python 3 library that supports the Raspberry Pi <a href="../accessories/build-hat.html">Build HAT</a>, run the following command:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">sudo </span>apt <span class="nb">install </span>python3-build-hat</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>To find Python packages distributed with <code>apt</code>, <a href="#search-for-software">use <code>apt search</code></a>. In most cases, Python packages use the prefix <code>python-</code> or <code>python3-</code>: for instance, you can find the <code>numpy</code> package under the name <code>python3-numpy</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect2">
<h3 id="install-python-libraries-using-pip">
<a class="anchor" href="#install-python-libraries-using-pip"></a>Install Python libraries using <code>pip</code>
</h3>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="python-on-raspberry-pi">
<a class="anchor" href="#python-on-raspberry-pi"></a><em>Bookworm</em> changes to <code>pip</code> installation</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>From Raspberry Pi OS <em>Bookworm</em> onwards, you cannot install libraries directly into the system version of Python.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Instead, <a href="#use-pip-with-virtual-environments">install libraries into a virtual environment (<code>venv</code>)</a>. To install a library at the system level for all users, <a href="#install-python-packages-using-apt">install it with <code>apt</code></a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Attempting to install a Python package system-wide outputs an error similar to the following:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span>pip <span class="nb">install </span>buildhat
<span class="go">error: externally-managed-environment

× This environment is externally managed
</span><span class="gp">╰─&gt;</span><span class="w"> </span>To <span class="nb">install </span>Python packages system-wide, try apt <span class="nb">install</span>
<span class="go">  python3-xyz, where xyz is the package you are trying to
  install.

  If you wish to install a non-Debian-packaged Python package,
  create a virtual environment using python3 -m venv path/to/venv.
  Then use path/to/venv/bin/python and path/to/venv/bin/pip. Make
  sure you have python3-full installed.

  For more information visit http://rptl.io/venv

note: If you believe this is a mistake, please contact your Python installation or OS distribution provider. You can override this, at the risk of breaking your Python installation or OS, by passing --break-system-packages.
hint: See PEP 668 for the detailed specification.</span></code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Python users have long dealt with conflicts between OS package managers like <code>apt</code> and Python-specific package management tools like <code>pip</code>. These conflicts include both Python-level API incompatibilities and conflicts over file ownership.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>From Raspberry Pi OS <em>Bookworm</em> onwards, packages installed through <code>pip</code> <em>must be installed into a Python virtual environment</em> (<code>venv</code>). A virtual environment is a container where you can safely install third-party modules so they won’t interfere with your system Python.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect3">
<h4 id="use-pip-with-virtual-environments">
<a class="anchor" href="#use-pip-with-virtual-environments"></a>Use <code>pip</code> with virtual environments</h4>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>To use a virtual environment, create a container to store the environment. There are several ways you can do this depending on how you want to work with Python:</p>
</div>
<div id="tabs-1" class="openblock tabs is-loading">
<div class="content">
<div class="ulist tablist">
<ul><li id="tabs-1-per-project-environments" class="tab">
<p>per-project environments</p>
</li>
<li id="tabs-1-per-user-environments" class="tab">
<p>per-user environments</p>
</li>
</ul></div>
<div id="tabs-1-per-project-environments--panel" class="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tabs-1-per-project-environments">
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Many users create separate virtual environments for each Python project. Locate the virtual environment in the root folder of each project, typically with a shared name like <code>env</code>. Run the following command from the root folder of each project to create a virtual environment configuration folder:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span>python <span class="nt">-m</span> venv <span class="nb">env</span></code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Before you work on a project, run the following command from the root of the project to start using the virtual environment:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">source env</span>/bin/activate</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>You should then see a prompt similar to the following:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">(env) $</span></code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>When you finish working on a project, run the following command from any directory to leave the virtual environment:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">(env) $</span><span class="w"> </span>deactivate</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div id="tabs-1-per-user-environments--panel" class="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tabs-1-per-user-environments">
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Instead of creating a virtual environment for each of your Python projects, you can create a single virtual environment for your user account. <strong>Activate that virtual environment before running any of your Python code.</strong> This approach can be more convenient for workflows that share many libraries across projects.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>When creating a virtual environment for multiple projects across an entire user account, consider locating the virtual environment configuration files in your home directory. Store your configuration in a <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hidden_file_and_hidden_directory#Unix_and_Unix-like_environments">folder whose name begins with a period</a> to hide the folder by default, preventing it from cluttering your home folder.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Use the following command to create a virtual environment in a hidden folder in the current user’s home directory:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span>python <span class="nt">-m</span> venv ~/.env</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Run the following command from any directory to start using the virtual environment:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">

</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">source</span> ~/.env/bin/activate</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>You should then see a prompt similar to the following:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">(.env) $</span></code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>To leave the virtual environment, run the following command from any directory:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>

<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">(.env) $</span><span class="w"> </span>deactivate</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect4">
<h5 id="create-a-virtual-environment">
<a class="anchor" href="#create-a-virtual-environment"></a>Create a virtual environment</h5>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Run the following command to create a virtual environment configuration folder, replacing <code>&lt;env-name&gt;</code> with the name you would like to use for the virtual environment (for example, <code>env</code>):</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span>python <span class="nt">-m</span> venv &lt;env-name&gt;</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="admonitionblock tip">
<table><tr><td class="icon">
<div class="title">Tip</div>
</td>
<td class="content">
Pass the <code>--system-site-packages</code> flag before the folder name to preload all of the currently installed packages in your system Python installation into the virtual environment.
</td>
</tr></table></div>
</div>
<div class="sect4">
<h5 id="enter-a-virtual-environment">
<a class="anchor" href="#enter-a-virtual-environment"></a>Enter a virtual environment</h5>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Then, execute the <code>bin/activate</code> script in the virtual environment configuration folder to enter the virtual environment:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">$</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">source</span> &lt;env-name&gt;/bin/activate</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>You should then see a prompt similar to the following:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">(&lt;env-name&gt;) $</span></code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>The <code>(&lt;env-name&gt;)</code> command prompt prefix indicates that the current terminal session is in a virtual environment named <code>&lt;env-name&gt;</code>.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>To check that you’re in a virtual environment, use <code>pip list</code> to view the list of installed packages:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="console"><span class="gp">(&lt;env-name&gt;) $</span><span class="w"> </span>pip list
<span class="go">Package    Version
---------- -------
pip        23.0.1
setuptools 66.1.1</span></code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>The list should be much shorter than the list of packages installed in your system Python. You can now safely install packages with <code>pip</code>. Any packages you install with <code>pip</code> while in a virtual environment only install to that virtual environment. In a virtual environment, the <code>python</code> or <code>python3</code> commands automatically use the virtual environment’s version of Python and installed packages instead of the system Python.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect4">
<h5 id="exit-a-virtual-environment">
<a class="anchor" href="#exit-a-virtual-environment"></a>Exit a virtual environment</h5>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>To leave a virtual environment, run the following command:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect2">
<h3 id="use-the-thonny-editor">
<a class="anchor" href="#use-the-thonny-editor"></a>Use the Thonny editor</h3>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>We recommend <a href="https://thonny.org/">Thonny</a> for editing Python code on the Raspberry Pi.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>By default, Thonny uses the system Python. However, you can switch to using a Python virtual environment by clicking on the <strong>interpreter menu</strong> in the bottom right of the Thonny window. Select a configured environment or configure a new virtual environment with <code>Configure interpreter…​</code>.</p>
</div>
<div class="imageblock">
<div class="content">
<img src="images/thonny-venv.jpg?hash=bd05f31b8fd072e3daa372cf0ae46d05" alt="thonny venv" width="100%"></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect1">
<h2 id="use-gpio-from-python">
<a class="anchor" href="#use-gpio-from-python"></a>Use GPIO from Python</h2>
<div class="sectionbody"></div>
<div class="paragraph edit-link">
<p>Edit this <a href="https://github.com/raspberrypi/documentation/blob/master/documentation/asciidoc/computers/os/using-gpio.adoc">on GitHub</a></p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Using the <a href="https://gpiozero.readthedocs.io/">GPIO Zero</a> library makes it easy to control GPIO devices with Python. The library is comprehensively documented at <a href="https://gpiozero.readthedocs.io/">gpiozero.readthedocs.io</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>For information about GPIO hardware, see <a href="../computers/raspberry-pi.html#gpio">GPIO hardware</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="sect2">
<h3 id="led-control">
<a class="anchor" href="#led-control"></a>LED control</h3>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>The following example code controls an LED connected to GPIO17:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="python"><span class="kn">from</span> <span class="n">gpiozero</span> <span class="kn">import</span> <span class="n">LED</span>
<span class="kn">from</span> <span class="n">time</span> <span class="kn">import</span> <span class="n">sleep</span>

<span class="n">led</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nc">LED</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">17</span><span class="p">)</span>

<span class="k">while</span> <span class="bp">True</span><span class="p">:</span>
    <span class="n">led</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">on</span><span class="p">()</span>
    <span class="nf">sleep</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)</span>
    <span class="n">led</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">off</span><span class="p">()</span>
    <span class="nf">sleep</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Run this in an IDE like Thonny, and the LED will blink on and off repeatedly.</p>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>LED methods include <code>on()</code>, <code>off()</code>, <code>toggle()</code>, and <code>blink()</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect2">
<h3 id="read-button-state">
<a class="anchor" href="#read-button-state"></a>Read button state</h3>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>The following example code reads the state of a button connected to GPIO2:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="python"><span class="kn">from</span> <span class="n">gpiozero</span> <span class="kn">import</span> <span class="n">Button</span>
<span class="kn">from</span> <span class="n">time</span> <span class="kn">import</span> <span class="n">sleep</span>

<span class="n">button</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nc">Button</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">)</span>

<span class="k">while</span> <span class="bp">True</span><span class="p">:</span>
    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">button</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">is_pressed</span><span class="p">:</span>
        <span class="nf">print</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="sh">"</span><span class="s">Pressed</span><span class="sh">"</span><span class="p">)</span>
    <span class="k">else</span><span class="p">:</span>
        <span class="nf">print</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="sh">"</span><span class="s">Released</span><span class="sh">"</span><span class="p">)</span>
    <span class="nf">sleep</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Button functionality includes the properties <code>is_pressed</code> and <code>is_held</code>; callbacks <code>when_pressed</code>, <code>when_released</code>, and <code>when_held</code>; and methods <code>wait_for_press()</code> and <code>wait_for_release</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sect2">
<h3 id="control-an-led-with-a-button">
<a class="anchor" href="#control-an-led-with-a-button"></a>Control an LED with a button</h3>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>The following example code reads the state of a button connected to GPIO2, and lights an LED connected to GPIO17 when the button is pressed:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" data-name="Layer 1" viewbox="0 0 12 12"><rect x="1" y="6" width="5" height="1"></rect><path d="M10,1H8A1,1,0,0,0,7,0H6A1,1,0,0,0,5,1H3A1,1,0,0,0,2,2V5H3V2H4V3H9V2h1v9H3V8H2v3a1,1,0,0,0,1,1h7a1,1,0,0,0,1-1V2A1,1,0,0,0,10,1ZM6.5,2.25a.75.75,0,1,1,.75-.75A.76.76,0,0,1,6.5,2.25Z"></path><path d="M6.32,6.32,4.43,4.43A.25.25,0,0,0,4,4.6V8.4a.25.25,0,0,0,.43.17L6.32,6.68A.25.25,0,0,0,6.32,6.32Z"></path></svg><strong class="copy-button-label"></strong><span class="tooltip hidden"> Copy to Clipboard</span>
</div></button>
<div class="content">
<pre class="rouge highlight"><code data-lang="python"><span class="kn">from</span> <span class="n">gpiozero</span> <span class="kn">import</span> <span class="n">LED</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">Button</span>

<span class="n">led</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nc">LED</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">17</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">button</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nc">Button</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">)</span>

<span class="k">while</span> <span class="bp">True</span><span class="p">:</span>
    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">button</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">is_pressed</span><span class="p">:</span>
        <span class="n">led</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">on</span><span class="p">()</span>
    <span class="k">else</span><span class="p">:</span>
        <span class="n">led</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">off</span><span class="p">()</span></code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="paragraph">
<p>Alternatively:</p>
</div>
<div class="listingblock">
<button class="copy-button hidden" aria-label="copy code to clipboard" type="button" aria-hidden="true"><div class="copy-button-inner">
